# OpenTake planning-document reconciliation — 2026-07-14

This report is the human-readable index for `requirements.json`. The JSON ledger is normative: it preserves every candidate ID, source location, disposition, implementation evidence, verification evidence, and acceptance criterion. Historical documents remain intact; appended calibration pointers direct current decisions here.

## Coverage and integrity

- Candidate records: 2704
- Unique candidate IDs: 2704
- Unverified dispositions: 0
- Requirements ledger SHA-256: `9d90b12e0cc2f19d737c514164b69e8e0927ed7095f65550c42e181c454e8552`
- Independent-review correction: all 298 earlier complete claims were re-evaluated without candidate-ID exceptions; 9 retain strict evidence and 289 were downgraded to source-bound active gaps with unique deterministic closure scenarios.
- Complete dispositions require at least one exact tracked implementation symbol and at least one exact tracked automated-test name. Self-reported runtime commit, hash, or receipt entries are rejected in documents scope.
- Incomplete dispositions require a subsystem gap group and an exact acceptance criterion. Contradicted, obsolete, and duplicate records retain source provenance and an explicit final disposition.

| Status | Records | Meaning |
|---|---:|---|
| complete | 9 | Current implementation and verification evidence support the statement. |
| contradicted | 53 | Current evidence disproves the source statement. |
| duplicate | 1955 | The candidate is context or repeats another adjudicated requirement. |
| incomplete | 557 | A real current gap remains with an exact closure contract. |
| obsolete | 130 | A newer decision or implementation supersedes the statement. |

## Source authority

Conflicts were resolved in this order: current architecture authority and current implementation/tests; module contracts; approved execution plans; upstream/parity references; historical execution records. A historical claim never overrides newer code and deterministic evidence.

|---|---:|
| approved execution plan | 310 |
| current architecture authority | 91 |
| historical execution record | 97 |
| module contract and specification | 1294 |
| supporting project documentation | 727 |

## Review provenance

Four non-overlapping review sets cover all 2,704 candidate IDs: modules/specifications, Superpowers and execution plans, architecture/port/historical non-heading signals, and independently reviewed remaining headings. Their evidence was normalized into the tracked ledger; the scratch review files are development inputs and are not runtime dependencies.

- Modules/specifications review SHA-256: `5e7f44cea25ed313ed7aec469bdcd731a50b979b5ca4dee994ff9acfaca46753`
- Superpowers/execution-plan review SHA-256: `8cd5e444fe9aee92073ab798b913c7241b42b0a207cd190f1f94fa0077ce62ed`
- Architecture/port/historical review SHA-256: `2fd89282cf8a90d13f19e02924ed987254ff01bdecd8675a623989af85b803cd`
- Remaining-heading review SHA-256: `1e917da84656c447d7dc0c8a1ed160080fb25e5f2153b501fcdd238e6fdaff8d`

The document verifier realpath-confines `document-candidates.json`, `requirements.json`, the Git index, and every current source/evidence file they reference. It fails closed on lexical or symlink escapes, tracked symlinks/non-files, identity/text/signal/heading drift, missing/orphan/duplicate identities, unsupported status or gap values, malformed schema fields, commented/string-only code claims, non-test Rust functions, non-literal TypeScript test aliases, any self-reported runtime evidence, and incomplete work without source-bound closure criteria.

Task 8 must exclude generated `docs/audit/**` Markdown from candidate discovery before regeneration; otherwise this report's gap rows would recursively create more audit-output candidates on every pass. The original 2,704 source candidates and relocation evidence remain normative inputs.
